# Link Index — External Sources Cited in the Welltech Intelligence Repository

A deduplicated click-through index of every external URL cited anywhere in this repository. Links are grouped by source type, then by domain; each entry carries a short label and the repository folder(s) that cite it. Folder tags: `00-exec`, `10-market`, `20-competitor`, `30-reviews`, `40-doctor`, `50-marketing`, `60-ai`, `70-blueprint`.

For annotated descriptions of what each source supports, see [bibliography.md](bibliography.md). For the compliance-instrument index see [regulations.md](regulations.md); for the quantitative sources see [datasets.md](datasets.md).

**Last updated: July 2026** · 3132 unique URLs across 1258 domains.

> Methodology note: many government and regulator PDFs were accessed via search-indexed content rather than direct download (several official hosts blocked direct retrieval in the research environment). Where a figure rests on search-extracted content, the citing document flags it; re-verify against the primary URL before external use.
